                    trim: '<circle cx="6" cy="6" r="3"/><circle cx="6" cy="18" r="3"/><path d="M8.5 8.5L19 19M19 5L14.5 9.5M8.5 15.5L6 18"/>',
                    split: '<path d="M4 12h4M16 12h4M11 5l-2 14M13 5l2 14"/>',
                    merge: '<path d="M6 3v7a3 3 0 0 0 3 3h6a3 3 0 0 0 3-3V3M12 13v8M8 17l4 4 4-4"/>',
                    crop: '<path d="M6 2v14a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h14M18 22V8a2 2 0 0 0-2-2H2"/>',
                    rotate: '<path d="M3 12a9 9 0 1 0 2.8-6.5"/><path d="M3 4v5h5"/>',
                    flip: '<path d="M12 3v18M6 8l-3 4 3 4M18 8l3 4-3 4"/>',
                    thumbnail: '<rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><circle cx="8.5" cy="8.5" r="1.5"/><path d="M21 15l-5-5L5 21"/>',
                    frames: '<rect x="3" y="3" width="7" height="7" rx="1"/><rect x="14" y="3" width="7" height="7" rx="1"/><rect x="3" y="14" width="7" height="7" rx="1"/><rect x="14" y="14" width="7" height="7" rx="1"/>',
                    gif: '<rect x="2.5" y="6" width="19" height="12" rx="2"/><path d="M9.5 10l4 2.5-4 2.5V10z"/>',
                    audioremove: '<path d="M11 5L6 9H2v6h4l5 4V5z"/><path d="M23 9l-6 6M17 9l6 6"/>',
                    audioreplace: '<path d="M9 18V5l12-2v13"/><circle cx="6" cy="18" r="3"/><circle cx="18" cy="16" r="3"/><path d="M3 8l3-3 3 3"/>',
                    watermark: '<path d="M12 2s6 6.5 6 11a6 6 0 0 1-12 0c0-4.5 6-11 6-11z"/>',
                    subtitle: '<rect x="2.5" y="5" width="19" height="14" rx="2"/><path d="M7 14h3M13 14h4M7 10.5h10"/>',
                    volume: '<path d="M11 5L6 9H2v6h4l5 4V5z"/><path d="M15.5 8.5a5 5 0 0 1 0 7M18.5 5.5a9 9 0 0 1 0 13"/>',
                    bitrate: '<path d="M2 12h4l2-7 4 14 2-7h4l2 3h2"/>',
                    samplerate: '<path d="M2 12c2-4 4-4 6 0s4 4 6 0 4-4 6 0"/>',
                    channels: '<circle cx="7" cy="12" r="4"/><circle cx="17" cy="12" r="4"/>',
                    metadata: '<path d="M20.5 12.5L12 21l-9-9 8.5-8.5H20.5v8.5z"/><circle cx="15.5" cy="7.5" r="1"/>',
                    extract: '<path d="M9 18V5l12-2v13"/><circle cx="6" cy="18" r="3"/><circle cx="18" cy="16" r="3"/><path d="M21 12V3"/>'
                  };
                  function svg(name, cls) {
                    return '<svg class="' + (cls || '') + '" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">' + (ICON[name] || '') + '</svg>';
                  }

                  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------
                     OPERATIONS — id, category, label, icon, short desc, minimal
                     field list, and a buildCommand(ctx) function returning the
                     exact ffmpeg invocation for the currently selected values.
                  --------------------------------------------------------------- */
                  function fx(file) { return file || 'input.mp4'; }
                  function outName(base, ext) { return base.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '') + '_out.' + ext; }

                  var RESOLUTION_PRESETS = {
                    '426×240 — 240p': [426, 240],
                    '640×360 — 360p': [640, 360],
                    '854×480 — 480p': [854, 480],
                    '1280×720 — 720p HD': [1280, 720],
                    '1600×900 — 900p': [1600, 900],
                    '1920×1080 — 1080p Full HD': [1920, 1080],
                    '2560×1440 — 1440p QHD': [2560, 1440],
                    '3840×2160 — 4K UHD': [3840, 2160],
                    '4096×2160 — 4K DCI': [4096, 2160],
                    '5120×2880 — 5K': [5120, 2880],
                    '7680×4320 — 8K UHD': [7680, 4320],
                    '1080×1920 — Vertical HD': [1080, 1920],
                    '1080×1080 — Square': [1080, 1080]
                  };

                  var VIDEO_CODEC_MAP = { 'H.264': 'libx264', 'H.265 (HEVC)': 'libx265', 'AV1': 'libsvtav1', 'VP9': 'libvpx-vp9', 'VP8': 'libvpx', 'MPEG-4': 'mpeg4', 'MPEG-2': 'mpeg2video', 'ProRes': 'prores_ks', 'Copy (no re-encode)': 'copy' };
                  var AUDIO_CODEC_MAP = { 'AAC': 'aac', 'MP3': 'libmp3lame', 'Opus': 'libopus', 'Vorbis': 'libvorbis', 'FLAC': 'flac', 'ALAC': 'alac', 'PCM (WAV)': 'pcm_s16le', 'AC3': 'ac3', 'Copy (no re-encode)': 'copy' };
                  var AUDIO_BITRATES = ['32k', '64k', '96k', '128k', '160k', '192k', '224k', '256k', '320k', '384k'];
                  var SAMPLE_RATES = ['8000 Hz', '11025 Hz', '16000 Hz', '22050 Hz', '32000 Hz', '44100 Hz', '48000 Hz', '88200 Hz', '96000 Hz', '176400 Hz', '192000 Hz'];

                  var OPS = [
                    {
                      id: 'format', cat: 'video', label: 'Convert', icon: 'format', desc: 'Change container & codec',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'outFormat', label: 'Output Format', type: 'select', options: ['MP4', 'MKV', 'MOV', 'AVI', 'WEBM', 'WMV', 'FLV', 'MPEG', 'TS', 'M4V', '3GP', 'OGV', 'GIF'], def: 'MP4' },
                        { id: 'vcodec', label: 'Video Codec', type: 'select', options: Object.keys(VIDEO_CODEC_MAP), def: 'H.264' },
                        { id: 'acodec', label: 'Audio Codec', type: 'select', options: Object.keys(AUDIO_CODEC_MAP), def: 'AAC' },
                        { id: 'quality', label: 'Quality (CRF)', type: 'range', min: 0, max: 51, def: 20 }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var vc = VIDEO_CODEC_MAP[v.vcodec];
                        var ac = AUDIO_CODEC_MAP[v.acodec];
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -c:v ' + vc + (vc !== 'copy' ? ' -crf ' + v.quality : '') + ' -c:a ' + ac + ' "' + outName(fx(f), v.outFormat.toLowerCase()) + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'resolution', cat: 'video', label: 'Resize', icon: 'resize', desc: 'Scale frame dimensions',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'preset', label: 'Resolution Preset', type: 'select', options: ['Custom'].concat(Object.keys(RESOLUTION_PRESETS)), def: 'Custom', presetMap: RESOLUTION_PRESETS },
                        { id: 'width', label: 'Width', type: 'number', def: 1280, unit: 'px' },
                        { id: 'height', label: 'Height', type: 'number', def: 720, unit: 'px' },
                        { id: 'scaling', label: 'Scaling Algorithm', type: 'select', options: ['Bilinear', 'Bicubic', 'Lanczos', 'Neighbor (Fast)', 'Spline'], def: 'Lanczos' },
                        { id: 'keepAspect', label: 'Keep Aspect Ratio', type: 'toggle', def: true, span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var h = v.keepAspect ? '-1' : v.height;
                        var flagMap = { 'Bilinear': 'bilinear', 'Bicubic': 'bicubic', 'Lanczos': 'lanczos', 'Neighbor (Fast)': 'neighbor', 'Spline': 'spline' };
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -vf "scale=' + v.width + ':' + h + ':flags=' + flagMap[v.scaling] + '" -c:a copy "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp4') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'fps', cat: 'video', label: 'Change FPS', icon: 'fps', desc: 'Retime the frame rate',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'target', label: 'Target FPS', type: 'select', options: ['12', '15', '23.976', '24', '25', '29.97', '30', '48', '50', '59.94', '60', '90', '100', '120', '144', '240'], def: '30' },
                        { id: 'mode', label: 'Frame Handling', type: 'select', options: ['Duplicate / Drop (default)', 'Motion Interpolation (smoother, slower)'], def: 'Duplicate / Drop (default)' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var vf = v.mode.indexOf('Interpolation') === 0 || v.mode.indexOf('Motion') === 0
                          ? ' -vf "minterpolate=fps=' + v.target + ':mi_mode=mci"'
                          : '';
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '"' + vf + ' -r ' + v.target + ' -c:a copy "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp4') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'compression', cat: 'video', label: 'Compress', icon: 'compress', desc: 'Reduce file size',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'quality', label: 'Quality', type: 'select', options: ['Lossless', 'Very High', 'High', 'Medium', 'Low', 'Very Low (smallest file)'], def: 'Medium' },
                        { id: 'codec', label: 'Codec', type: 'select', options: ['H.264', 'H.265 (HEVC)', 'AV1', 'VP9'], def: 'H.264' },
                        { id: 'preset', label: 'Encoding Speed', type: 'select', options: ['Ultrafast', 'Superfast', 'Fast', 'Medium', 'Slow', 'Veryslow (best compression)'], def: 'Medium' },
                        { id: 'hw', label: 'Hardware Encoder', type: 'select', options: ['Auto', 'NVIDIA NVENC', 'Intel Quick Sync', 'AMD AMF', 'None (CPU)'], def: 'Auto', span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var crf = { Lossless: 0, 'Very High': 16, High: 20, Medium: 24, Low: 30, 'Very Low (smallest file)': 36 }[v.quality];
                        var codecMap = { 'H.264': 'libx264', 'H.265 (HEVC)': 'libx265', 'AV1': 'libsvtav1', 'VP9': 'libvpx-vp9' };
                        var vc = codecMap[v.codec];
                        var extra = v.codec === 'VP9' ? ' -b:v 0' : '';
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -c:v ' + vc + ' -crf ' + crf + extra + ' -preset ' + v.preset.toLowerCase().split(' ')[0] + ' -c:a aac -b:a 128k "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp4') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'trim', cat: 'video', label: 'Trim', icon: 'trim', desc: 'Cut to an in/out range',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'start', label: 'Start Time', type: 'text', def: '00:00:10' },
                        { id: 'end', label: 'End Time', type: 'text', def: '00:01:30' },
                        { id: 'mode', label: 'Trim Mode', type: 'segmented', options: ['Copy (Fast)', 'Re-encode (Accurate)'], def: 'Copy (Fast)', span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        if (v.mode === 'Re-encode (Accurate)') {
                          return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -ss ' + v.start + ' -to ' + v.end + ' -c:v libx264 -crf 18 -c:a aac "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp4') + '"';
                        }
                        return 'ffmpeg -ss ' + v.start + ' -to ' + v.end + ' -i "' + fx(f) + '" -c copy "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp4') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'split', cat: 'video', label: 'Split', icon: 'split', desc: 'Break into equal segments',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'seconds', label: 'Segment Length', type: 'number', def: 60, unit: 'sec' },
                        { id: 'reset', label: 'Reset Timestamps per Segment', type: 'toggle', def: true, span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -c copy -map 0 -segment_time ' + v.seconds + (v.reset ? ' -reset_timestamps 1' : '') + ' -f segment "' + fx(f).replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '') + '_%03d.mp4"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'merge', cat: 'video', label: 'Merge', icon: 'merge', desc: 'Join checked files in order',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'reencode', label: 'Re-encode (fixes mismatched codecs)', type: 'toggle', def: false, span2: true },
                        { id: 'transition', label: 'Transition', type: 'select', options: ['None', 'Fade (0.5s)', 'Crossfade (1s)'], def: 'None' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f, files) {
                        var mode = v.reencode || v.transition !== 'None' ? '-c:v libx264 -c:a aac' : '-c copy';
                        var note = v.transition !== 'None' ? '  # ' + v.transition + ' via xfade/acrossfade' : '';
                        return 'ffmpeg -f concat -safe 0 -i filelist.txt ' + mode + ' "merged_output.mp4"' + note + '  # ' + (files || []).length + ' files listed';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'crop', cat: 'video', label: 'Crop', icon: 'crop', desc: 'Trim visible frame edges',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'preset', label: 'Aspect Preset', type: 'select', options: ['Custom', '1:1 Square', '4:3 Classic', '16:9 Widescreen', '9:16 Vertical', '21:9 Ultrawide'], def: 'Custom' },
                        { id: 'width', label: 'Width', type: 'number', def: 1080, unit: 'px' },
                        { id: 'height', label: 'Height', type: 'number', def: 1080, unit: 'px' },
                        { id: 'x', label: 'X Offset', type: 'number', def: 420, unit: 'px' },
                        { id: 'y', label: 'Y Offset', type: 'number', def: 0, unit: 'px' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -vf "crop=' + v.width + ':' + v.height + ':' + v.x + ':' + v.y + '" -c:a copy "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp4') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'rotate', cat: 'video', label: 'Rotate', icon: 'rotate', desc: 'Rotate the frame',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'angle', label: 'Angle', type: 'select', options: ['90° Clockwise', '90° Counter-clockwise', '180°', '270°', 'Custom'], def: '90° Clockwise' },
                        { id: 'custom', label: 'Custom Angle', type: 'number', def: 45, unit: '°' },
                        { id: 'autoRotate', label: 'Auto-Rotate from Metadata', type: 'toggle', def: true, span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var map = { '90° Clockwise': 'transpose=1', '90° Counter-clockwise': 'transpose=2', '180°': 'transpose=1,transpose=1', '270°': 'transpose=2,transpose=2,transpose=2' };
                        var flt = map[v.angle] || ('rotate=' + v.custom + '*PI/180');
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -vf "' + flt + '" -c:a copy "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp4') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'flip', cat: 'video', label: 'Flip', icon: 'flip', desc: 'Mirror the frame',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'dir', label: 'Direction', type: 'segmented', options: ['Horizontal', 'Vertical', 'Both'], def: 'Horizontal', span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var flt = { Horizontal: 'hflip', Vertical: 'vflip', Both: 'hflip,vflip' }[v.dir];
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -vf ' + flt + ' -c:a copy "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp4') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'thumbnail', cat: 'video', label: 'Thumbnail', icon: 'thumbnail', desc: 'Grab a single frame',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'time', label: 'Capture Time', type: 'text', def: '00:00:05' },
                        { id: 'format', label: 'Format', type: 'select', options: ['PNG', 'JPG', 'WEBP', 'BMP', 'TIFF'], def: 'PNG' },
                        { id: 'quality', label: 'Quality', type: 'range', min: 1, max: 100, def: 95, span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var q = ' -q:v ' + Math.max(1, Math.round((100 - v.quality) / 3));
                        return 'ffmpeg -ss ' + v.time + ' -i "' + fx(f) + '" -frames:v 1' + q + ' "' + outName(fx(f), v.format.toLowerCase()) + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'frames', cat: 'video', label: 'Extract Frames', icon: 'frames', desc: 'Pull multiple frames',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'mode', label: 'Extract', type: 'select', options: ['Every Frame', 'Every Second', 'Every 2 Seconds', 'Every 5 Seconds', 'Every 10 Frames', 'Every 30 Frames'], def: 'Every Second' },
                        { id: 'format', label: 'Format', type: 'select', options: ['PNG', 'JPG', 'WEBP', 'BMP'], def: 'PNG' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var vfMap = { 'Every Frame': null, 'Every Second': 'fps=1', 'Every 2 Seconds': 'fps=0.5', 'Every 5 Seconds': 'fps=0.2', 'Every 10 Frames': "select='not(mod(n\\,10))'", 'Every 30 Frames': "select='not(mod(n\\,30))'" };
                        var vf = vfMap[v.mode];
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '"' + (vf ? ' -vf "' + vf + '"' : '') + ' "frame_%04d.' + v.format.toLowerCase() + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'gif', cat: 'video', label: 'Create GIF', icon: 'gif', desc: 'Clip to an animated GIF',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'start', label: 'Start', type: 'text', def: '00:00:02' },
                        { id: 'end', label: 'End', type: 'text', def: '00:00:06' },
                        { id: 'fps', label: 'FPS', type: 'number', def: 12 },
                        { id: 'width', label: 'Width', type: 'number', def: 480, unit: 'px' },
                        { id: 'loop', label: 'Loop', type: 'select', options: ['Infinite Loop', 'Play Once'], def: 'Infinite Loop' },
                        { id: 'dither', label: 'Dithering', type: 'select', options: ['Bayer', 'Floyd-Steinberg', 'Sierra2', 'None'], def: 'Floyd-Steinberg' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var loopFlag = v.loop === 'Infinite Loop' ? '0' : '-1';
                        var ditherMap = { Bayer: 'bayer', 'Floyd-Steinberg': 'floyd_steinberg', Sierra2: 'sierra2', None: 'none' };
                        return 'ffmpeg -ss ' + v.start + ' -to ' + v.end + ' -i "' + fx(f) + '" -vf "fps=' + v.fps + ',scale=' + v.width + ':-1:flags=lanczos,split[s0][s1];[s0]palettegen[p];[s1][p]paletteuse=dither=' + ditherMap[v.dither] + '" -loop ' + loopFlag + ' "' + outName(fx(f), 'gif') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'audioremove', cat: 'video', label: 'Remove Audio', icon: 'audioremove', desc: 'Strip the audio track — no extra settings needed',
                      fields: [],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -c:v copy -an "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp4') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'audioreplace', cat: 'video', label: 'Replace Audio', icon: 'audioreplace', desc: 'Swap in a new audio track',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'audioFile', label: 'Audio File', type: 'file', def: 'new_track.mp3', span2: true },
                        { id: 'sync', label: 'Duration Match', type: 'select', options: ['Shortest Stream', 'Match Video Length'], def: 'Shortest Stream' },
                        { id: 'volume', label: 'Volume', type: 'range', min: -20, max: 20, def: 0, unit: 'dB' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var flag = v.sync === 'Shortest Stream' ? ' -shortest' : '';
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -i "' + v.audioFile + '" -filter:a "volume=' + v.volume + 'dB" -map 0:v -map 1:a -c:v copy' + flag + ' "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp4') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'watermark', cat: 'video', label: 'Watermark', icon: 'watermark', desc: 'Overlay a logo image',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'image', label: 'Watermark Image', type: 'file', def: 'logo.png', span2: true },
                        { id: 'position', label: 'Position', type: 'select', options: ['Top Left', 'Top Right', 'Bottom Left', 'Bottom Right', 'Center'], def: 'Bottom Right' },
                        { id: 'scale', label: 'Scale', type: 'number', def: 20, unit: '%' },
                        { id: 'opacity', label: 'Opacity', type: 'range', min: 0, max: 100, def: 80, unit: '%' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var pos = { 'Top Left': '10:10', 'Top Right': 'W-w-10:10', 'Bottom Left': '10:H-h-10', 'Bottom Right': 'W-w-10:H-h-10', 'Center': '(W-w)/2:(H-h)/2' }[v.position];
                        var scaleF = (v.scale / 100).toFixed(2);
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -i "' + v.image + '" -filter_complex "[1]scale=iw*' + scaleF + ':-1,format=rgba,colorchannelmixer=aa=' + (v.opacity / 100).toFixed(2) + '[wm];[0][wm]overlay=' + pos + '" "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp4') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'subtitle', cat: 'video', label: 'Burn Subtitles', icon: 'subtitle', desc: 'Hard-code a subtitle file',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'file', label: 'Subtitle File', type: 'file', def: 'captions.srt', span2: true },
                        { id: 'size', label: 'Font Size', type: 'number', def: 24 },
                        { id: 'color', label: 'Font Color', type: 'color', def: '#FFFFFF' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var hex = (v.color || '#FFFFFF').replace('#', '');
                        var assColor = '&H00' + hex.slice(4, 6) + hex.slice(2, 4) + hex.slice(0, 2);
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -vf "subtitles=\'' + v.file + '\':force_style=\'FontSize=' + v.size + ',PrimaryColour=' + assColor + '\'" "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp4') + '"';
                      }
                    },

                    /* -------- AUDIO -------- */
                    {
                      id: 'a-format', cat: 'audio', label: 'Convert', icon: 'format', desc: 'Change audio format',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'outFormat', label: 'Output Format', type: 'select', options: ['MP3', 'WAV', 'AAC', 'FLAC', 'OGG', 'M4A', 'WMA', 'OPUS'], def: 'MP3' },
                        { id: 'bitrate', label: 'Bitrate', type: 'select', options: AUDIO_BITRATES, def: '192k' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var codec = { MP3: 'libmp3lame', WAV: 'pcm_s16le', AAC: 'aac', FLAC: 'flac', OGG: 'libvorbis', M4A: 'aac', WMA: 'wmav2', OPUS: 'libopus' }[v.outFormat];
                        var lossless = v.outFormat === 'WAV' || v.outFormat === 'FLAC';
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -c:a ' + codec + (!lossless ? ' -b:a ' + v.bitrate : '') + ' "' + outName(fx(f), v.outFormat.toLowerCase()) + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'a-merge', cat: 'audio', label: 'Merge', icon: 'merge', desc: 'Join checked files in order',
                      fields: [],
                      build: function (v, f, files) {
                        return 'ffmpeg -f concat -safe 0 -i filelist.txt -c copy "merged_audio.mp3"  # ' + (files || []).length + ' files listed';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'a-split', cat: 'audio', label: 'Split', icon: 'split', desc: 'Break into equal segments',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'seconds', label: 'Segment Length', type: 'number', def: 120, unit: 'sec' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -f segment -segment_time ' + v.seconds + ' -c copy "' + fx(f).replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '') + '_%03d.mp3"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'a-extract', cat: 'audio', label: 'Extract from Video', icon: 'extract', desc: 'Pull the audio track out',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'outFormat', label: 'Output Format', type: 'select', options: ['MP3', 'AAC', 'WAV', 'FLAC', 'OGG', 'M4A', 'OPUS'], def: 'MP3' },
                        { id: 'bitrate', label: 'Bitrate', type: 'select', options: AUDIO_BITRATES, def: '192k' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var codec = { MP3: 'libmp3lame', AAC: 'aac', WAV: 'pcm_s16le', FLAC: 'flac', OGG: 'libvorbis', M4A: 'aac', OPUS: 'libopus' }[v.outFormat];
                        var lossless = v.outFormat === 'WAV' || v.outFormat === 'FLAC';
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -vn -c:a ' + codec + (!lossless ? ' -b:a ' + v.bitrate : '') + ' "' + outName(fx(f), v.outFormat.toLowerCase()) + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'a-volume', cat: 'audio', label: 'Volume', icon: 'volume', desc: 'Boost or lower gain',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'gain', label: 'Gain', type: 'range', min: -20, max: 20, def: 0, unit: 'dB', span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -filter:a "volume=' + v.gain + 'dB" "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp3') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'a-bitrate', cat: 'audio', label: 'Bitrate', icon: 'bitrate', desc: 'Set a fixed output bitrate',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'bitrate', label: 'Target Bitrate', type: 'select', options: AUDIO_BITRATES, def: '192k' },
                        { id: 'vbr', label: 'Mode', type: 'segmented', options: ['CBR', 'VBR'], def: 'CBR' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var flag = v.vbr === 'VBR' ? ' -q:a 2' : ' -b:a ' + v.bitrate;
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '"' + flag + ' "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp3') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'a-samplerate', cat: 'audio', label: 'Sample Rate', icon: 'samplerate', desc: 'Resample the audio',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'rate', label: 'Sample Rate', type: 'select', options: SAMPLE_RATES, def: '44100 Hz', span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -ar ' + parseInt(v.rate, 10) + ' "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp3') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'a-channels', cat: 'audio', label: 'Channels', icon: 'channels', desc: 'Channel layout output',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'mode', label: 'Channel Layout', type: 'select', options: ['Mono (1.0)', 'Stereo (2.0)', '2.1', 'Quad (4.0)', '5.0', '5.1', '6.1', '7.1'], def: 'Stereo (2.0)', span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var map = { 'Mono (1.0)': 1, 'Stereo (2.0)': 2, '2.1': 3, 'Quad (4.0)': 4, '5.0': 5, '5.1': 6, '6.1': 7, '7.1': 8 };
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -ac ' + map[v.mode] + ' "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp3') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'a-metadata', cat: 'audio', label: 'Metadata', icon: 'metadata', desc: 'Edit ID3 tags',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'title', label: 'Title', type: 'text', def: 'Untitled Track' },
                        { id: 'artist', label: 'Artist', type: 'text', def: 'Unknown Artist' },
                        { id: 'album', label: 'Album', type: 'text', def: '' },
                        { id: 'genre', label: 'Genre', type: 'text', def: '' },
                        { id: 'year', label: 'Year', type: 'text', def: '' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var tags = ' -metadata title="' + v.title + '" -metadata artist="' + v.artist + '"';
                        if (v.album) tags += ' -metadata album="' + v.album + '"';
                        if (v.genre) tags += ' -metadata genre="' + v.genre + '"';
                        if (v.year) tags += ' -metadata date="' + v.year + '"';
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '"' + tags + ' -c copy "' + outName(fx(f), 'mp3') + '"';
                      }
                    },

                    /* -------- IMAGE -------- */
                    {
                      id: 'i-format', cat: 'image', label: 'Convert', icon: 'format', desc: 'Change image format',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'outFormat', label: 'Output Format', type: 'select', options: ['PNG', 'JPG', 'WEBP', 'BMP', 'TIFF', 'GIF', 'ICO', 'TGA'], def: 'PNG' },
                        { id: 'quality', label: 'Quality', type: 'range', min: 1, max: 100, def: 90 }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -q:v ' + Math.max(1, Math.round((100 - v.quality) / 3)) + ' "' + outName(fx(f), v.outFormat.toLowerCase()) + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'i-resize', cat: 'image', label: 'Resize', icon: 'resize', desc: 'Scale image dimensions',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'preset', label: 'Resolution Preset', type: 'select', options: ['Custom'].concat(Object.keys(RESOLUTION_PRESETS)), def: 'Custom', presetMap: RESOLUTION_PRESETS },
                        { id: 'width', label: 'Width', type: 'number', def: 1600, unit: 'px' },
                        { id: 'height', label: 'Height', type: 'number', def: 900, unit: 'px' },
                        { id: 'keepAspect', label: 'Keep Aspect Ratio', type: 'toggle', def: true, span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var h = v.keepAspect ? '-1' : v.height;
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -vf "scale=' + v.width + ':' + h + '" "' + outName(fx(f), 'png') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'i-compress', cat: 'image', label: 'Compress', icon: 'compress', desc: 'Reduce file size',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'quality', label: 'Quality', type: 'range', min: 1, max: 100, def: 75, span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -q:v ' + Math.max(1, Math.round((100 - v.quality) / 3)) + ' "' + outName(fx(f), 'jpg') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'i-rotate', cat: 'image', label: 'Rotate', icon: 'rotate', desc: 'Rotate the image',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'angle', label: 'Angle', type: 'select', options: ['90° Clockwise', '90° Counter-clockwise', '180°', '270°', 'Custom'], def: '90° Clockwise' },
                        { id: 'custom', label: 'Custom Angle', type: 'number', def: 45, unit: '°', span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var map = { '90° Clockwise': 'transpose=1', '90° Counter-clockwise': 'transpose=2', '180°': 'transpose=1,transpose=1', '270°': 'transpose=2,transpose=2,transpose=2' };
                        var flt = map[v.angle] || ('rotate=' + v.custom + '*PI/180');
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -vf "' + flt + '" "' + outName(fx(f), 'png') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'i-flip', cat: 'image', label: 'Flip', icon: 'flip', desc: 'Mirror the image',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'dir', label: 'Direction', type: 'segmented', options: ['Horizontal', 'Vertical', 'Both'], def: 'Horizontal', span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var flt = { Horizontal: 'hflip', Vertical: 'vflip', Both: 'hflip,vflip' }[v.dir];
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -vf ' + flt + ' "' + outName(fx(f), 'png') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'i-watermark', cat: 'image', label: 'Watermark', icon: 'watermark', desc: 'Overlay a logo image',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'image', label: 'Watermark Image', type: 'file', def: 'logo.png', span2: true },
                        { id: 'position', label: 'Position', type: 'select', options: ['Top Left', 'Top Right', 'Bottom Left', 'Bottom Right', 'Center'], def: 'Bottom Right' },
                        { id: 'scale', label: 'Scale', type: 'number', def: 20, unit: '%' },
                        { id: 'opacity', label: 'Opacity', type: 'range', min: 0, max: 100, def: 80, unit: '%' }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var pos = { 'Top Left': '10:10', 'Top Right': 'W-w-10:10', 'Bottom Left': '10:H-h-10', 'Bottom Right': 'W-w-10:H-h-10', 'Center': '(W-w)/2:(H-h)/2' }[v.position];
                        var scaleF = (v.scale / 100).toFixed(2);
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '" -i "' + v.image + '" -filter_complex "[1]scale=iw*' + scaleF + ':-1,format=rgba,colorchannelmixer=aa=' + (v.opacity / 100).toFixed(2) + '[wm];[0][wm]overlay=' + pos + '" "' + outName(fx(f), 'png') + '"';
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      id: 'i-metadata', cat: 'image', label: 'Metadata', icon: 'metadata', desc: 'Edit EXIF tags',
                      fields: [
                        { id: 'title', label: 'Title', type: 'text', def: '' },
                        { id: 'author', label: 'Author', type: 'text', def: '' },
                        { id: 'copyright', label: 'Copyright', type: 'text', def: '' },
                        { id: 'description', label: 'Description', type: 'text', def: '', span2: true }
                      ],
                      build: function (v, f) {
                        var tags = ' -metadata title="' + v.title + '" -metadata author="' + v.author + '"';
                        if (v.copyright) tags += ' -metadata copyright="' + v.copyright + '"';
                        if (v.description) tags += ' -metadata description="' + v.description + '"';
                        return 'ffmpeg -i "' + fx(f) + '"' + tags + ' "' + outName(fx(f), 'png') + '"';
                      }
                    }
                  ];

                  function findOp(id) { for (var i = 0; i < OPS.length; i++) if (OPS[i].id === id) return OPS[i]; return null; }

                  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------
                     STATE (UPDATED: No Fake Data, Dynamic Folder)
                  --------------------------------------------------------------- */

                  const os = require('os');
                  const path = require('path');
                  const defaultOut = path.join(os.homedir(), 'Videos', 'MediaStudio');

                  var state = {
                    category: 'video',
                    activeOp: 'format',
                    values: {},
                    files: [],
                    nextFileId: 1,
                    outputFolder: defaultOut,
                    queue: [],
                    nextJobId: 1,
                    isRunning: false,
                    isPaused: false
                  };

                  // UI তে ডাইনামিক ফোল্ডার নামটা সেট করে দেওয়া
                  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
                    const outFolderEl = document.getElementById('msOutputFolder');
                    if (outFolderEl) outFolderEl.textContent = defaultOut;
                  });

                  function escHtml(s) { return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
                  function fmtTime() { var d = new Date(); function p(n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; } return p(d.getHours()) + ':' + p(d.getMinutes()) + ':' + p(d.getSeconds()); }

                  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------
                     RENDER: operation strip
                  --------------------------------------------------------------- */
                  function renderOpStrip() {
                    var el = document.getElementById('msOpStrip');
                    if (!el) return;
                    var ops = OPS.filter(function (o) { return o.cat === state.category; });
                    el.innerHTML = ops.map(function (o) {
                      var isActive = o.id === state.activeOp;
                      var cls = isActive ? ' active just-selected' : '';
                      return '<button class="ms-op-card' + cls + '" data-op="' + o.id + '" onclick="MediaStudio.selectOp(\'' + o.id + '\')">' +
                        '<span class="ms-op-card-icon">' + svg(o.icon) + '</span>' +
                        '<span class="ms-op-card-label">' + o.label + '</span>' +
                        '</button>';
                    }).join('');
                  }

                  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------
                     RENDER: minimal field form for the active operation
                  --------------------------------------------------------------- */
                  function fieldControlHtml(op, field) {
                    var val = state.values[field.id];
                    var oninput = "MediaStudio.onFieldChange()";
                    var id = 'msf-' + field.id;
                    switch (field.type) {
                      case 'select':
                        var selChange = field.presetMap ? "MediaStudio.onPresetChange('" + field.id + "', this.value)" : oninput;
                        return '<div class="ms-select-wrap"><select class="ms-select" id="' + id + '" onchange="' + selChange + '">' +
                          field.options.map(function (o) { return '<option' + (o === val ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o + '</option>'; }).join('') +
                          '</select></div>';
                      case 'number':
                        return '<div class="ms-unit-input"><input class="ms-input" type="number" id="' + id + '" value="' + val + '" oninput="' + oninput + '">' +
                          (field.unit ? '<span class="ms-unit-suffix">' + field.unit + '</span>' : '') + '</div>';
                      case 'text':
                        return '<input class="ms-input" type="text" id="' + id + '" value="' + escHtml(val) + '" oninput="' + oninput + '">';
                      case 'toggle':
                        return '<label class="ms-toggle"><input type="checkbox" id="' + id + '"' + (val ? ' checked' : '') + ' onchange="' + oninput + '"><span class="ms-toggle-track"></span><span class="ms-toggle-thumb"></span></label>';
                      case 'range':
                        return '<div class="ms-range-row"><input type="range" class="ms-range" id="' + id + '" min="' + field.min + '" max="' + field.max + '" value="' + val + '" oninput="MediaStudio.onRangeInput(\'' + field.id + '\')"><span class="ms-range-value" id="' + id + '-val">' + val + (field.unit || '') + '</span></div>';
                      case 'segmented':
                        return '<div class="ms-segmented" id="' + id + '"><span class="ms-segmented-indicator"></span>' + field.options.map(function (o) {
                          return '<button type="button" class="' + (o === val ? 'active' : '') + '" onclick="MediaStudio.onSegmentChange(\'' + field.id + '\', this)">' + o + '</button>';
                        }).join('') + '</div>';
                      case 'file':
                        return '<div class="ms-file-row"><span class="ms-file-row-name' + (val ? '' : ' is-empty') + '" id="' + id + '-name">' + (val || 'No file chosen') + '</span><button type="button" class="ms-browse-btn" onclick="MediaStudio.browseAuxFile(\'' + field.id + '\')">Browse</button></div>';
                      case 'color':
                        return '<span class="ms-color-swatch"><input type="color" id="' + id + '" value="' + (val || '#FFFFFF') + '" onchange="' + oninput + '"></span>';
                      default:
                        return '';
                    }
                  }

                  function onPresetChange(fieldId, value) {
                    var op = findOp(state.activeOp);
                    var field = op.fields.filter(function (f) { return f.id === fieldId; })[0];
                    state.values[fieldId] = value;
                    if (field && field.presetMap && field.presetMap[value]) {
                      state.values.width = field.presetMap[value][0];
                      state.values.height = field.presetMap[value][1];
                    }
                    renderForm();
                  }

                  function renderForm() {
                    var op = findOp(state.activeOp);
                    var el = document.getElementById('msOpForm');
                    if (!op || !el) return;

                    // seed default values for any field not already set for this op
                    op.fields.forEach(function (f) { if (!(f.id in state.values)) state.values[f.id] = f.def; });

                    var html = '<div class="ms-op-form-head"><span class="ms-op-form-icon">' + svg(op.icon) + '</span>' +
                      '<div class="ms-op-form-head-text"><div class="ms-op-form-title">' + op.label + '</div><div class="ms-op-form-desc">' + op.desc + '</div></div></div>';

                    if (op.fields.length === 0) {
                      html += '<p class="text-caption" style="color:var(--text-tertiary); line-height:17px;">Nothing to configure — this operation runs with sensible defaults.</p>';
                    } else {
                      html += '<div class="ms-op-fields">';
                      html += op.fields.map(function (f) {
                        return '<div class="ms-field"><span class="ms-field-label">' + f.label + '</span>' + fieldControlHtml(op, f) + '</div>';
                      }).join('');
                      html += '</div>';
                    }

                    el.innerHTML = html;
                    positionAllSegmentIndicators(el);
                    updateCommand();
                  }

                  function onFieldChange() {
                    var op = findOp(state.activeOp);
                    if (!op) return;
                    op.fields.forEach(function (f) {
                      var el = document.getElementById('msf-' + f.id);
                      if (!el) return;
                      if (f.type === 'toggle') state.values[f.id] = el.checked;
                      else if (f.type === 'number' || f.type === 'range') state.values[f.id] = parseFloat(el.value);
                      else state.values[f.id] = el.value;
                    });
                    updateCommand();
                  }

                  function onRangeInput(fieldId) {
                    var op = findOp(state.activeOp);
                    var field = op.fields.filter(function (f) { return f.id === fieldId; })[0];
                    var el = document.getElementById('msf-' + fieldId);
                    var valEl = document.getElementById('msf-' + fieldId + '-val');
                    state.values[fieldId] = parseFloat(el.value);
                    if (valEl) valEl.textContent = el.value + (field.unit || '');
                    updateCommand();
                  }

                  function positionSegmentIndicator(wrap, skipAnim) {
                    if (!wrap) return;
                    var indicator = wrap.querySelector('.ms-segmented-indicator');
                    var active = wrap.querySelector('button.active');
                    if (!indicator || !active) return;
                    if (skipAnim) indicator.style.transition = 'none';
                    indicator.style.left = active.offsetLeft + 'px';
                    indicator.style.width = active.offsetWidth + 'px';
                    if (skipAnim) {
                      indicator.offsetHeight;
                      indicator.style.transition = '';
                    }
                  }

                  function positionAllSegmentIndicators(root) {
                    var wraps = (root || document).querySelectorAll('.ms-segmented');
                    Array.prototype.forEach.call(wraps, function (w) { positionSegmentIndicator(w, true); });
                  }

                  function onSegmentChange(fieldId, btn) {
                    var wrap = btn.parentNode;
                    Array.prototype.forEach.call(wrap.children, function (b) { b.classList.remove('active'); });
                    btn.classList.add('active');
                    state.values[fieldId] = btn.textContent;
                    positionSegmentIndicator(wrap, false);
                    updateCommand();
                  }

                  function browseAuxFile(fieldId) {
                    var name = prompt('Enter a file name (this simulates a native file picker):', state.values[fieldId] || '');
                    if (name === null) return;
                    state.values[fieldId] = name;
                    var nameEl = document.getElementById('msf-' + fieldId + '-name');
                    if (nameEl) { nameEl.textContent = name || 'No file chosen'; nameEl.classList.toggle('is-empty', !name); }
                    updateCommand();
                  }

                  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------
                     COMMAND PREVIEW
                  --------------------------------------------------------------- */
                  function primaryFile() {
                    var checked = state.files.filter(function (f) { return f.checked; });
                    return checked.length ? checked[0].name : (state.files[0] ? state.files[0].name : 'input.mp4');
                  }

                  var commandFlashTimer = null;
                  function updateCommand() {
                    var op = findOp(state.activeOp);
                    var el = document.getElementById('msCommandText');
                    if (!op || !el) return;
                    var checkedFiles = state.files.filter(function (f) { return f.checked; }).map(function (f) { return f.name; });
                    var cmd;
                    try {
                      cmd = op.build(state.values, primaryFile(), checkedFiles);
                    } catch (e) {
                      cmd = 'ffmpeg -i "' + primaryFile() + '" ...';
                    }
                    var changed = el.textContent !== cmd;
                    el.textContent = cmd;

                    if (changed) {
                      var box = document.getElementById('msCommandBox');
                      if (box) {
                        box.classList.add('is-updated');
                        clearTimeout(commandFlashTimer);
                        commandFlashTimer = setTimeout(function () { box.classList.remove('is-updated'); }, 420);
                      }
                    }
                  }

                  function copyCommand() {
                    var text = document.getElementById('msCommandText').textContent;
                    var btn = document.getElementById('msCommandCopyBtn');
                    var done = function () {
                      log('completed', 'Command copied to clipboard.');
                      if (btn) {
                        btn.classList.add('is-copied');
                        setTimeout(function () { btn.classList.remove('is-copied'); }, 1400);
                      }
                    };
                    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
                      navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(done, done);
                    } else { done(); }
                  }

                  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------
                     CATEGORY / OPERATION SELECTION
                  --------------------------------------------------------------- */
                  function positionCatIndicator(skipAnim) {
                    var wrap = document.getElementById('msCatTabs');
                    var indicator = document.getElementById('msCatIndicator');
                    if (!wrap || !indicator) return;
                    var active = wrap.querySelector('.ms-cat-tab.active');
                    if (!active) return;
                    if (skipAnim) indicator.style.transition = 'none';
                    indicator.style.left = active.offsetLeft + 'px';
                    indicator.style.width = active.offsetWidth + 'px';
                    if (skipAnim) {
                      indicator.offsetHeight; /* force reflow so the snap applies before transitions resume */
                      indicator.style.transition = '';
                    }
                  }

                  function setCategory(cat) {
                    state.category = cat;
                    var tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#msCatTabs .ms-cat-tab');
                    Array.prototype.forEach.call(tabs, function (t) { t.classList.toggle('active', t.getAttribute('data-cat') === cat); });
                    positionCatIndicator();
                    var firstOfCat = OPS.filter(function (o) { return o.cat === cat; })[0];
                    var newId = firstOfCat ? firstOfCat.id : null;
                    if (newId !== state.activeOp) state.values = {};
                    state.activeOp = newId;
                    renderOpStrip();
                    renderForm();
                  }

                  function selectOp(id) {
                    if (id !== state.activeOp) state.values = {};
                    state.activeOp = id;
                    renderOpStrip();
                    renderForm();
                  }

                  function resetForm() {
                    var op = findOp(state.activeOp);
                    if (!op) return;
                    op.fields.forEach(function (f) { state.values[f.id] = f.def; });
                    renderForm();
                  }

                  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------
                     FILE LIST
                  --------------------------------------------------------------- */
                  function fileIcon(kind) {
                    if (kind === 'audio') return '<path d="M9 18V5l12-2v13"/><circle cx="6" cy="18" r="3"/><circle cx="18" cy="16" r="3"/>';
                    if (kind === 'image') return '<rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><circle cx="8.5" cy="8.5" r="1.5"/><path d="M21 15l-5-5L5 21"/>';
                    return '<rect x="2.5" y="4.5" width="19" height="13" rx="2"/><path d="M9.5 8l4 2.5-4 2.5V8z"/>';
                  }

                  function renderFileList() {
                    var el = document.getElementById('msFileList');
                    if (!el) return;
                    el.innerHTML = state.files.map(function (f) {
                      return '<div class="ms-file-item">' +
                        '<input type="checkbox" ' + (f.checked ? 'checked' : '') + ' onchange="MediaStudio.toggleFile(' + f.id + ')">' +
                        '<span class="ms-file-item-icon"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">' + fileIcon(f.kind) + '</svg></span>' +
                        '<span class="ms-file-item-name">' + escHtml(f.name) + '</span>' +
                        '<span class="ms-file-item-size">' + f.size + '</span>' +
                        '<button class="ms-file-item-remove" onclick="MediaStudio.removeFile(' + f.id + ')"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/></svg></button>' +
                        '</div>';
                    }).join('');
                    updateCommand();
                  }

                  function toggleFile(id) {
                    state.files.forEach(function (f) { if (f.id === id) f.checked = !f.checked; });
                    updateCommand();
                  }

                  function removeFile(id) {
                    state.files = state.files.filter(function (f) { return f.id !== id; });
                    renderFileList();
                  }

                  function guessKind(name) {
                    var ext = (name.split('.').pop() || '').toLowerCase();
                    if (['mp3', 'wav', 'aac', 'flac', 'ogg', 'm4a'].indexOf(ext) !== -1) return 'audio';
                    if (['png', 'jpg', 'jpeg', 'webp', 'bmp', 'gif', 'tiff'].indexOf(ext) !== -1) return 'image';
                    return 'video';
                  }

                  function fmtBytes(n) {
                    if (n > 1e9) return (n / 1e9).toFixed(1) + ' GB';
                    if (n > 1e6) return (n / 1e6).toFixed(1) + ' MB';
                    if (n > 1e3) return (n / 1e3).toFixed(1) + ' KB';
                    return n + ' B';
                  }

                  // 🔴 NEW: Electron Native File Picker (আসল ফাইল লোকেশন পাবে)
                  async function importFiles() {
                    if (typeof require !== 'undefined') {
                      const { ipcRenderer } = require('electron');
                      const fs = require('fs');
                      try {
                        const filePaths = await ipcRenderer.invoke('dialog:openFiles');
                        if (filePaths && filePaths.length > 0) {
                          filePaths.forEach(filePath => {
                            const stats = fs.statSync(filePath);
                            const fileName = filePath.split('\\').pop().split('/').pop();

                            // ডুপ্লিকেট ফাইল আটকানো
                            if (!state.files.find(f => f.absolutePath === filePath)) {
                              state.files.push({
                                id: state.nextFileId++,
                                name: fileName,
                                absolutePath: filePath, // 🔴 REAL PATH
                                size: fmtBytes(stats.size),
                                kind: guessKind(fileName),
                                checked: true
                              });
                              log('info', 'Added file: ' + fileName);
                            }
                          });
                          renderFileList();
                          updateCommand();
                        }
                      } catch (err) {
                        log('error', 'Could not open file dialog.');
                      }
                    }
                  }

                  function browseOutputFolder() {
                    var p = prompt('Output folder (simulates a native folder picker):', state.outputFolder);
                    if (p === null || p === '') return;
                    state.outputFolder = p;
                    document.getElementById('msOutputFolder').textContent = p;
                  }

                  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------
                     CONSOLE
                  --------------------------------------------------------------- */
                  var CONSOLE_MAX_LINES = 100;
                  var CONSOLE_BOTTOM_THRESHOLD = 28;

                  function isConsoleAtBottom(el) {
                    return (el.scrollHeight - el.scrollTop - el.clientHeight) <= CONSOLE_BOTTOM_THRESHOLD;
                  }

                  function log(tag, text) {
                    var el = document.getElementById('msConsole');
                    if (!el) return;
                    var prompt = document.getElementById('msConsolePrompt');
                    var wasAtBottom = isConsoleAtBottom(el);

                    var row = document.createElement('div');
                    row.className = 'console-line';
                    row.innerHTML = '<span class="console-time">' + fmtTime() + '</span><span class="console-tag ' + tag + '">' + tag.toUpperCase() + '</span><span class="console-text"></span>';
                    row.querySelector('.console-text').textContent = text;
                    if (prompt) el.insertBefore(row, prompt); else el.appendChild(row);

                    var lines = el.querySelectorAll('.console-line');
                    var overflowCount = lines.length - CONSOLE_MAX_LINES;
                    for (var i = 0; i < overflowCount; i++) {
                      lines[i].remove();
                    }

                    if (wasAtBottom) {
                      el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight;
                    }
                  }

                  function clearConsole() {
                    var el = document.getElementById('msConsole');
                    if (!el) return;
                    var lines = el.querySelectorAll('.console-line');
                    lines.forEach(function (l) { l.remove(); });
                  }

                  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------
                     QUEUE / EXECUTION
                  --------------------------------------------------------------- */
                  // ==========================================
                  // 🔴 AUTO-SAVE & CRASH RECOVERY LOGIC
                  // ==========================================
                  function saveQueueState() {
                    localStorage.setItem('ms_queue_state', JSON.stringify({
                      queue: state.queue,
                      outputFolder: state.outputFolder,
                      nextJobId: state.nextJobId
                    }));
                  }

                  function loadQueueState() {
                    try {
                      var saved = localStorage.getItem('ms_queue_state');
                      if (saved) {
                        var parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
                        if (parsed.queue) state.queue = parsed.queue;
                        if (parsed.outputFolder) state.outputFolder = parsed.outputFolder;
                        if (parsed.nextJobId) state.nextJobId = parsed.nextJobId;

                        // কারেন্ট যাওয়ার সময় যেটা রানিং ছিল, সেটাকে 'waiting' করে দিলাম, যাতে আবার রান করা যায়
                        var needsSave = false;
                        state.queue.forEach(function (j) {
                          if (j.status === 'running') {
                            j.status = 'waiting';
                            j.progress = 0;
                            needsSave = true;
                          }
                        });
                        if (needsSave) saveQueueState();
                      }
                    } catch (e) { }
                  }

                  // স্ক্রিপ্ট লোড হওয়ার সাথে সাথেই আগের ডাটা রিস্টোর করবে
                  loadQueueState();

                  // ==========================================
                  // 🔴 ORIGINAL FUNCTIONS (UPDATED WITH SAVE & IPC)
                  // ==========================================
                  function queueCounts() {
                    var c = { waiting: 0, running: 0, completed: 0, failed: 0 };
                    state.queue.forEach(function (j) { c[j.status]++; });
                    return c;
                  }

                  function updateReadouts() {
                    var c = queueCounts();
                    document.getElementById('msQueueChip').textContent = state.queue.length + ' in queue';
                    document.getElementById('msQueueReadout').textContent = c.waiting + ' waiting · ' + c.running + ' running';
                    var statusEl = document.getElementById('msStatusReadout');
                    var dot = document.getElementById('msStatusDot');
                    if (state.isRunning && !state.isPaused) {
                      statusEl.className = 'readout-value state-running'; statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="chip-dot connecting" id="msStatusDot"></span>Running';
                    } else if (state.isPaused) {
                      statusEl.className = 'readout-value state-paused'; statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="chip-dot" id="msStatusDot"></span>Paused';
                    } else if (c.waiting === 0 && c.running === 0 && state.queue.length > 0) {
                      statusEl.className = 'readout-value state-complete'; statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="chip-dot connected" id="msStatusDot"></span>Complete';
                    } else {
                      statusEl.className = 'readout-value state-idle'; statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="chip-dot" id="msStatusDot"></span>Idle';
                    }
                    document.getElementById('msPauseBtn').disabled = !state.isRunning;
                    document.getElementById('msPauseBtn').querySelector('span').textContent = state.isPaused ? 'Resume' : 'Pause';
                  }

                  function statusPillHtml(status) {
                    var label = status.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + status.slice(1);
                    return '<span class="status-pill ' + status + '">' + label + '</span>';
                  }

                  function renderQueue() {
                    var el = document.getElementById('msQueueList');
                    if (!el) return;
                    if (state.queue.length === 0) {
                      el.innerHTML = '<div class="ms-queue-empty-row"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 6h16M4 12h16M4 18h10"/></svg>No jobs yet — add one from the operation builder above.</div>';
                      updateReadouts();
                      return;
                    }
                    var fillColor = { running: 'var(--accent-primary)', waiting: 'var(--text-tertiary)', completed: 'var(--color-success)', failed: 'var(--color-error)' };
                    el.innerHTML = state.queue.slice().reverse().map(function (j) {
                      var actions = '';
                      if (j.status === 'failed') {
                        actions += '<button class="ms-queue-row-btn" data-tooltip="Retry" onclick="MediaStudio.retryJob(' + j.id + ')"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M3 12a9 9 0 1 0 2.8-6.5"/><path d="M3 4v5h5"/></svg></button>';
                      }
                      if (j.status === 'waiting' || j.status === 'failed' || j.status === 'completed') {
                        actions += '<button class="ms-queue-row-btn" data-tooltip="Remove" onclick="MediaStudio.removeJob(' + j.id + ')"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/></svg></button>';
                      }
                      return '<div class="ms-queue-row" data-status="' + j.status + '">' +
                        '<span class="ms-queue-row-icon"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">' + fileIcon(j.kind) + '</svg></span>' +
                        '<div class="ms-queue-row-main"><div class="ms-queue-row-name">' + escHtml(j.fileName) + '</div><div class="ms-queue-row-sub">' + escHtml(j.opLabel) + '</div></div>' +
                        '<div class="ms-queue-row-progress"><div class="progress-track"><div class="progress-fill" style="width:' + j.progress + '%; background:' + fillColor[j.status] + ';"></div></div><span class="ms-queue-row-pct">' + j.progress + '%</span></div>' +
                        statusPillHtml(j.status) +
                        '<div class="ms-queue-row-actions">' + actions + '</div>' +
                        '</div>';
                    }).join('');
                    updateReadouts();
                  }

                  function buildJobsFromSelection() {
                    var op = findOp(state.activeOp);
                    var checked = state.files.filter(function (f) { return f.checked; });
                    if (checked.length === 0) { log('error', 'No input files selected — check at least one file first.'); return []; }
                    return checked.map(function (f) {
                      var cmd = op.build(state.values, f.name, checked.map(function (x) { return x.name; }));
                      return { id: state.nextJobId++, fileName: f.name, kind: f.kind, opLabel: op.label, command: cmd, status: 'waiting', progress: 0 };
                    });
                  }

                  function addToQueue() {
                    var jobs = buildJobsFromSelection();
                    if (!jobs.length) return;
                    jobs.forEach(function (j) {
                      state.queue.push(j);
                      log('waiting', 'Queued "' + j.opLabel + '" → ' + j.fileName);
                    });
                    renderQueue();
                    saveQueueState(); // 🔴 Auto Save
                    processQueue();
                  }

                  function runNow() {
                    var jobs = buildJobsFromSelection();
                    if (!jobs.length) return;
                    jobs.forEach(function (j) { state.queue.push(j); });
                    renderQueue();
                    saveQueueState(); // 🔴 Auto Save
                    log('sent', 'Run requested for ' + jobs.length + ' file(s). Executing real FFmpeg...');

                    var btn = document.getElementById('msRunBtn');
                    if (btn && !btn.classList.contains('is-loading')) {
                      var original = btn.innerHTML;
                      btn.classList.add('is-loading');
                      btn.innerHTML = '<svg class="btn-spinner" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M12 3a9 9 0 1 0 9 9"/></svg><span>Starting…</span>';
                      setTimeout(function () {
                        btn.classList.remove('is-loading');
                        btn.innerHTML = original;
                        processQueue();
                      }, 480);
                    } else {
                      processQueue();
                    }
                  }

                  function processQueue() {
                    if (state.isPaused) return;
                    if (state.isRunning) return;
                    var next = state.queue.filter(function (j) { return j.status === 'waiting'; })[0];
                    if (!next) { state.isRunning = false; updateReadouts(); return; }

                    state.isRunning = true;
                    next.status = 'running';
                    renderQueue();
                    saveQueueState(); // 🔴 Auto Save

                    log('sent', 'CMD  ' + next.command);
                    log('info', 'Executing Real FFmpeg Engine for Job #' + next.id);

                    // 🔴 REAL IPC FFMPEG TRIGGER
                    if (typeof require !== 'undefined') {
                      const { ipcRenderer } = require('electron');
                      ipcRenderer.send('run-media-studio-ffmpeg', {
                        jobId: next.id,
                        command: next.command,
                        outputFolder: state.outputFolder
                      });
                    } else {
                      log('error', 'Electron IPC not available. Cannot run FFmpeg.');
                      state.isRunning = false;
                    }
                  }

                  function pauseQueue() {
                    state.isPaused = !state.isPaused;
                    log('info', state.isPaused ? 'Queue paused.' : 'Queue resumed.');
                    updateReadouts();
                    if (!state.isPaused) processQueue();
                  }

                  function resetQueue() {
                    state.queue = [];
                    state.isRunning = false;
                    state.isPaused = false;
                    renderQueue();
                    saveQueueState(); // 🔴 Auto Save (Clears Memory)
                    log('info', 'Queue and memory cleared.');
                  }

                  function retryJob(id) {
                    state.queue.forEach(function (j) { if (j.id === id) { j.status = 'waiting'; j.progress = 0; } });
                    renderQueue();
                    saveQueueState(); // 🔴 Auto Save
                    processQueue();
                  }

                  function retryFailed() {
                    var any = false;
                    state.queue.forEach(function (j) { if (j.status === 'failed') { j.status = 'waiting'; j.progress = 0; any = true; } });
                    if (any) {
                      renderQueue();
                      saveQueueState(); // 🔴 Auto Save
                      processQueue();
                    }
                  }

                  function removeJob(id) {
                    state.queue = state.queue.filter(function (j) { return j.id !== id; });
                    renderQueue();
                    saveQueueState(); // 🔴 Auto Save
                  }

                  // ==========================================
                  // 🔴 IPC LISTENERS FOR REAL FFMPEG
                  // ==========================================
                  if (typeof require !== 'undefined' && !window.msIpcLoaded) {
                    window.msIpcLoaded = true; // Ensures listeners are added only once
                    const { ipcRenderer, shell } = require('electron');

                    ipcRenderer.on('ms-ffmpeg-missing', (event, data) => {
                      log('error', '⚠️ FFmpeg is not installed or not added to System PATH!');
                      log('info', 'Redirecting to the official FFmpeg download page...');

                      var job = state.queue.find(function (j) { return j.id === data.jobId; });
                      if (job) {
                        job.status = 'failed';
                        renderQueue();
                        saveQueueState();
                      }
                      state.isRunning = false;
                      shell.openExternal('https://ffmpeg.org/download.html#build-windows');
                    });

                    ipcRenderer.on('ms-console-log', (event, data) => {
                      log(data.type || 'info', data.text);
                    });

                    ipcRenderer.on('ms-job-progress', (event, data) => {
                      var job = state.queue.find(function (j) { return j.id === data.jobId; });
                      if (job && job.status === 'running') {
                        // Smooth progress increment
                        if (job.progress < 95) job.progress += 2;
                        renderQueue();
                        saveQueueState();
                      }
                    });

                    ipcRenderer.on('ms-job-complete', (event, data) => {
                      var job = state.queue.find(function (j) { return j.id === data.jobId; });
                      if (job) {
                        job.status = data.success ? 'completed' : 'failed';
                        job.progress = data.success ? 100 : job.progress;

                        if (data.success) {
                          var outPath = state.outputFolder + '\\' + job.fileName.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '') + '_out.mp4';
                          log('completed', 'Done → ' + outPath);
                          document.getElementById('msLastOutputRow').style.display = '';
                          document.getElementById('msLastOutputPath').textContent = outPath;
                        } else {
                          log('error', 'FFmpeg Process Failed or Interrupted.');
                        }
                        saveQueueState();
                      }

                      state.isRunning = false;
                      renderQueue();

                      // Auto-start next job
                      setTimeout(processQueue, 500);
                    });
                  }

                  function clearCompleted() {
                    state.queue = state.queue.filter(function (j) { return j.status !== 'completed'; });
                    renderQueue();
                  }

                  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------
                     INIT
                  --------------------------------------------------------------- */
                  function init() {
                    renderOpStrip();
                    renderForm();
                    renderFileList();
                    renderQueue();
                    positionCatIndicator(true);
                    log('ready', 'Media Studio ready. FFmpeg 6.1 detected on PATH.');
                  }

                  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
                  } else {
                    init();
                  }

                  window.MediaStudio = {
                    setCategory: setCategory,
                    selectOp: selectOp,
                    onFieldChange: onFieldChange,
                    onRangeInput: onRangeInput,
                    onSegmentChange: onSegmentChange,
                    onPresetChange: onPresetChange,
                    positionCatIndicator: positionCatIndicator,
                    positionAllSegmentIndicators: positionAllSegmentIndicators,
                    browseAuxFile: browseAuxFile,
                    resetForm: resetForm,
                    copyCommand: copyCommand,
                    importFiles: importFiles,
                    toggleFile: toggleFile,
                    removeFile: removeFile,
                    browseOutputFolder: browseOutputFolder,
                    addToQueue: addToQueue,
                    runNow: runNow,
                    pauseQueue: pauseQueue,
                    resetQueue: resetQueue,
                    retryJob: retryJob,
                    retryFailed: retryFailed,
                    removeJob: removeJob,
                    clearCompleted: clearCompleted,
                    clearConsole: clearConsole
                  };
                })();
              </script>
              <script>
                // Keep Media Studio's sliding indicators (category tabs, segmented
                // fields) correctly positioned. Needed because this page starts
                // display:none — offsetLeft/offsetWidth read as 0 while hidden, so
                // positions must be recomputed the moment switchPage() reveals it.
                (function () {
                  var target = document.getElementById('page-media-studio');
                  if (!target) return;

                  function reposition() {
                    if (!window.MediaStudio) return;
                    window.MediaStudio.positionCatIndicator(true);
                    window.MediaStudio.positionAllSegmentIndicators();
                  }

                  if (window.MutationObserver) {
                    var lastVisible = target.style.display !== 'none';
                    var mo = new MutationObserver(function () {
                      var isVisible = target.style.display !== 'none';
                      if (isVisible && !lastVisible) reposition();
                      lastVisible = isVisible;
                    });
                    mo.observe(target, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['style'] });
                  }

                  var resizeTimer = null;
                  window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
                    clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
                    resizeTimer = setTimeout(reposition, 80);
                  });
                })();
              </script>
            </div>
